7.8 Geography, heritage and awards: static facts in the news

☆ Static one-liners from June–July 2026
  • NCR: about 55,083 sq km; Delhi, 13 districts of Haryana, 8 of Uttar Pradesh, 2 of Rajasthan
  • Rakhigarhi: largest known Harappan town; others are Harappa, Mohenjodaro, Ganweriwala (Pakistan) and Dholavira (Gujarat)
  • Snow leopard (Panthera uncia): 12 range countries; Vulnerable on the IUCN Red List; State animal of Ladakh and Himachal Pradesh
  • Mekedatu: Cauvery balancing reservoir near Kanakapura, Ramanagara district, Karnataka
  • India–Nepal border 1,751 km; five Indian States; disputes: Kalapani–Limpiyadhura–Lipulekh (Uttarakhand) and Susta (Bihar)
  • Pacific Ocean: largest; Mariana Trench 11,034 m deepest; Strait of Hormuz links the Persian Gulf to the Gulf of Oman
  • Narmada: also Reva; longest west-flowing river; rises near Amarkantak; empties into the Gulf of Khambhat; Sardar Sarovar, Indira Sagar, Omkareshwar, Maheshwar
  • Loktak Lake: Moirang, Manipur; largest freshwater lake in the North-East; phumdis and Keibul Lamjao; Ramsar 1990; Montreux Record 1993
  • Black Sea: Bosphorus to Sea of Marmara; Kerch Strait to Sea of Azov; six coastal countries (Bulgaria, Ukraine, Russia, Georgia, Romania, Türkiye)
  • Jnanpith Award: established 1961; ₹11 lakh, bronze Vagdevi; any Eighth Schedule language
  • Muga silk (Assam): GI tag in 2007
  • Tarapur units 1 and 2 (1969, Maharashtra): oldest operating commercial reactors in the world
  • Mettur Dam: Kaveri, Salem; Salal Dam: Chenab, Reasi (J&K); Mullaperiyar: Periyar, Idukki (Kerala–Tamil Nadu dispute)
  • Nehru Trophy Boat Race: second Saturday of August, Punnamada Lake, Alappuzha
  • Sariska Tiger Reserve is in the Aravallis of Rajasthan; Nagarjunasagar–Srisailam is India's largest tiger reserve (Andhra Pradesh)
  • Sahariya tribe: PVTG in Madhya Pradesh, Rajasthan and Chhattisgarh; Kuno National Park in the Gwalior–Chambal region
  • Indian Navy: coastline about 11,098 km, EEZ about 2.4 million sq km, sea lanes carrying about 90% of trade by volume; MoD "Decade of Transformation" 2023–2032
  • UN International Year of the Woman Farmer: 2026
  • MECON granted Miniratna Category-I status

Memory tricks

Chain: PNB fraud 2018 → IL&FS 2018 → PMC 2019 → Yes Bank 2020 → LVB 2020.

★ Yes Bank reconstruction (March 2020)
  • 5 Mar 2020 → Centre placed Yes Bank under moratorium (RBI request) until 3 Apr; withdrawals capped at ₹50,000 per depositor; RBI superseded the board and appointed Prashant Kumar (ex-SBI CFO) as administrator
  • 6 Mar 2020 → RBI released the draft Yes Bank Ltd. Reconstruction Scheme, 2020
  • 13 Mar 2020 → Cabinet approved; scheme came into force the same day
  • SBI as investor bank → up to 49%, invested ₹7,250 crore (725 crore shares at ₹10); lock-in of 3 years, cannot fall below 26% within that time; other lenders (ICICI, HDFC, Axis, Kotak, Bandhan) joined
  • Moratorium lifted at 6 pm on 18 Mar 2020; Prashant Kumar became MD and CEO

"5-6-13-18 March": moratorium, draft, scheme, lifted. "49-26-3".

What was the withdrawal cap, SBI's stake and the lock-in?

8.3 Tax and disinvestment

★ Corporate tax cut of Sep 2019

In 2019 the Government cut the corporate tax rate to revive growth; this card gives the new rate and the legal provision.

  • Taxation Laws (Amendment) Ordinance, 20 Sep 2019 (later an Act) → announced by the Finance Minister to revive growth
  • Section 115BAA → option for domestic companies: 22% base (effective 25.17% with 10% surcharge and 4% cess); no exemptions; no MAT
  • Section 115BAB → 15% (effective 17.16%) for new manufacturing companies incorporated on or after 1 Oct 2019 and starting production before 31 Mar 2023
  • MAT for others cut from 18.5% to 15% | estimated revenue loss about ₹1.45 lakh crore
Memory tricks

"22 and 15; 25.17 and 17.16".

Why it exists

  • Fiscal consolidation and capital reallocation require unlocking value locked up in mature public sector commercial assets.
  • Private equity participation improves enterprise corporate governance, market discipline, and operational efficiency.

How it works

  • The designated department formulates disinvestment proposals, selects transaction advisors, and values enterprise shares.
  • Equity is sold through stock exchange offers for sale, institutional placements, or strategic privatization transfers.
  • Asset monetisation concessions lease operational infrastructure rights to long-term institutional investors.

More fixed facts

  • Disinvestment and asset monetisation target ₹80,000 crore for FY27 (FY26 revised estimate ₹33,837 crore); IDBI Bank and LIC as big-ticket sources
  • Minority stake sales in 9 PSUs ₹55,757 cr; InvIT monetisation ₹6,367 cr
  • After deal: Government 15% + LIC 19% = 34% combined.
  • LIC 6.5% → ₹31,515 cr; Coal India 2% ₹5,542 cr; NHPC 6.01% ₹4,357 cr; Hindustan Copper 6% ₹3,041 cr; proceeds under Miscellaneous Capital Receipts

How it changed

  • September 2026: Minority stake sales in 9 PSUs ₹55,757 cr; InvIT monetisation ₹6,367 cr

Traps

  • Asset monetisation conveys time-bound operational concession rights rather than permanent ownership transfer of core infrastructure.
  • Disinvestment proceeds accrue to public capital accounts to support capital development outlays.

In the news

  • Oct 2026: IDBI Bank: Cabinet approves sale to Fairfax arm
  • Sep 2026: Disinvestment ₹62,124 cr (78% of ₹80,000 cr FY27 target)
  • Jan Mar 2026: National Monetisation Pipeline 2.0 (NMP 2.0)
★ LIC IPO (May 2022)

LIC's IPO in May 2022 was India's largest at that time; this card gives the dates, the price and the stake sold.

  • Subscription 4–9 May 2022 | listed on BSE and NSE 17 May 2022 | price band ₹902–949 (issue price ₹949)
  • Offer for sale of about 3.5% stake (22.13 crore shares), cut from the planned 5% | raised ₹20,557 crore (issue size ₹21,008 crore before discounts) → largest IPO in India at the time, ahead of Paytm (₹18,300 crore, Nov 2021)
  • Run by DIPAM (Department of Investment and Public Asset Management); Secretary then: Tuhin Kanta Pandey
  • LIC was formed 1956 by merging 245 private insurers (LIC Act 1956)
Memory tricks

"4-9 May, list 17 May, 3.5%".

8.4 India on the world stage

★ G20 Presidency, BRICS expansion and space

India held the G20 Presidency in 2023, and BRICS expanded; the card gives the dates, themes and key facts.

  • India's G20 Presidency → 1 Dec 2022 to 30 Nov 2023 | 18th Summit, 9–10 Sep 2023, Bharat Mandapam, New Delhi | theme "One Earth, One Family, One Future" (Vasudhaiva Kutumbakam)
  • Outcomes → New Delhi Leaders' Declaration adopted by consensus; African Union made a permanent member (21st member, 9 Sep 2023); Global Biofuels Alliance launched
  • BRICS expansion → decided at the Johannesburg Summit (Aug 2023); Egypt, Ethiopia, Iran, Saudi Arabia, UAE joined from 1 Jan 2024; Indonesia joined Jan 2025 (11 members); partner-country category created at Kazan (Oct 2024)
  • Chandrayaan-3 → launched 14 Jul 2023; Vikram lander touched down near the lunar south pole on 23 Aug 2023 (India: 4th country to soft-land on the Moon, first near the south pole); Chandrayaan-2 (2019) failed at landing
  • Aditya-L1 → solar mission launched 2 Sep 2023
Memory tricks

G20 "1 Dec 22 → 9-10 Sep 23, AU = 21st". Chandrayaan: launch 14 July, land 23 August.

★ Foreign Trade Policy and export promotion schemes
In plain words: Government policy frameworks and incentive mechanisms designed to expand merchandise and services exports and integrate global value chains.

Why it exists

  • Export growth generates foreign exchange earnings, creates domestic manufacturing jobs, and enhances industrial competitiveness.
  • Compensating exporters for unrebated domestic taxes ensures domestic products remain price-competitive in overseas markets.

How it works

  • Trade authorities issue strategic trade guidelines and duty remission entitlements for export-oriented manufacturing units.
  • Exporters claim input tax remissions, duty exemptions on imported capital goods, and export credit guarantee coverage.
  • Regional export promotion councils facilitate international buyer connections and address logistical bottlenecks.

Fixed facts

  • April–August 2026-27: merchandise exports $215.91 bn, imports $363.00 bn; services exports $183.36 bn, imports $96.65 bn; total trade balance −$60.38 bn
  • District Export Action Plans: 590 draft, 249 adopted (Mar 2026); phased roll-out from 1 June 2026 in 27 States/UTs
  • Remission of Duties and Taxes on Exported Products (RoDTEP, from 2021): extended 3 months (DGFT); rates unchanged (refund 0.3%–3.9%); budget ₹ 10,000 cr in FY27 (₹18,232 cr in 2025-26).

How it changed

  • April 2026: Remission of Duties and Taxes on Exported Products (RoDTEP, from 2021): extended 3 months (DGFT); rates unchanged (refund 0.3%–3.9%); budget ₹ 10,000 cr in FY27 (₹18,232 cr in 2025-26).

Traps

  • Export promotion schemes rebate un-refunded local duties without providing prohibited direct production subsidies.
  • District export initiatives coordinate trade logistics rather than functioning as separate capital grants.

In the news

  • Sep 2026: Exports in August 2026: US$82.68 bn
  • Sep 2026: Districts as Export Hubs
  • Sep 2026: RBI keeps 9-month export-realisation limit; withdraws 15-month relaxation
  • Oct 2026: India trade data, April–September 2026-27
  • Apr 2026: PMI and exports
  • Apr 2026: Manufacturing hubs and export target
  • Apr 2026: China becomes India's top trading partner in FY26
  • Apr 2026: Exports and trade balance, FY2025-26
  • Apr 2026: RELIEF scheme for exporters (West Asia disruption)
  • Apr 2026: RoDTEP, RoSCTL and SEZ-to-DTA relief
  • Apr 2026: Textile exports FY2025-26
  • Jan Mar 2026: CGSE operational
  • Jan Mar 2026: RELIEF: export support amid West Asia disruptions
★ Foreign Direct Investment policy and routing
In plain words: The regulatory regime governing cross-border equity capital investments by non-resident entities in domestic enterprises.

Why it exists

  • Long-term foreign capital brings advanced technological expertise, managerial know-how, and stable productive investment into domestic industries.
  • Calibrated sectoral investment caps protect sensitive national security interests while encouraging economic modernization.

How it works

  • Foreign enterprises invest under automatic routes requiring post-facto intimation or approval routes requiring government scrutiny.
  • Inward remittances are converted into equity or convertible debentures complying with statutory valuation benchmarks.
  • The central bank and designated departments monitor foreign shareholding limits and sectoral compliance filings.

Fixed facts

  • Finance Ministry notification under FEMA: overseas firms with Chinese shareholding up to 10% may invest through the automatic route
  • Finance Ministry notification: 100% FDI in insurance under automatic route; insurance intermediaries 100%
  • E-commerce companies can hold inventory for export purposes
  • Condition: goods must be manufactured or produced in India

How it changed

  • September 2026: E-commerce companies can hold inventory for export purposes
  • May 2026: Finance Ministry notification under FEMA: overseas firms with Chinese shareholding up to 10% may invest through the automatic route

Traps

  • Automatic routing permits foreign investments without prior ministerial sanction, subject to sectoral caps.
  • Direct investment implies long-term operational control rather than short-term secondary market portfolio trading.

In the news

  • Oct 2026: Japan tops India's FDI chart, Q1 FY27 (Apr–Jun 2026)
  • Sep 2026: FDI rules: e-commerce firms may hold inventory for exports
  • Sep 2026: Record FDI inflow of $94.53 bn in 2025–26
  • May 2026: FDI from land-border countries: up to 10% Chinese stake eased
  • Apr 2026: FDI equity inflows, April-December FY26
  • Jan Mar 2026: LBC investment guidelines eased (Cabinet)
★ Sovereign credit ratings and growth forecasting
In plain words: Independent institutional evaluations assessing the creditworthiness of a sovereign government and its future economic growth trajectory.

Why it exists

  • Global institutional investors require objective comparative risk assessments before deploying international capital into sovereign securities.
  • Favourable sovereign credit evaluations reduce international borrowing costs for the government and domestic corporate borrowers.

How it works

  • Rating agencies evaluate macroeconomic growth prospects, fiscal deficits, sovereign debt ratios, and external financial stability.
  • Agencies assign credit ratings ranging from prime investment grade down to speculative speculative distress grades.
  • International multilateral institutions publish periodic regional growth forecasts reflecting underlying structural trends.

Fixed facts

  • Country ceiling also raised one notch to A
  • First return to the ‘A’ category in 35+ years
  • Last ‘A’ rating: Moody’s A2 in 1988, lost in the 1990–91 Balance of Payments (BoP) crisis

How it changed

  • September 2026: Country ceiling also raised one notch to A
  • September 2026: First return to the ‘A’ category in 35+ years

Traps

  • Sovereign ratings evaluate government default probability rather than short-term stock market price movements.
  • A country credit ceiling sets the benchmark upper limit for foreign currency debt ratings of domestic entities.

In the news

  • Sep 2026: JCR upgrades India’s sovereign rating to A-
  • Apr 2026: India growth forecasts: World Bank, ADB, IMF, UN ESCAP, Moody's (April 2026)
  • Jan Mar 2026: Growth forecasts for India: UN, World Bank, IMF (Jan 2026)

9.3 Indices and look-alikes

☆ Base years of key indices
IndexBase yearNote
CPI (new series)2024Replaced base 2012; first used for January 2026 data (released Feb 2026); basket items 299 → 358 (goods 259 → 308, services 40 → 50); 12 divisions; weights from the 2023-24 Household Consumption Expenditure Survey; food weight 45.86% → 36.75%
GDP / National Accounts2022-23Replaced 2011-12; new series published 27 Feb 2026
IIP (Index of Industrial Production)2022-23Replaced 2011-12; first release 1 Jun 2026 (April 2026 data, 4.9%); 1,042 products mapped to 463 item groups; 120 groups added and 64 dropped (e.g. kerosene, CFLs); quick estimates on the 28th
WPI (Wholesale Price Index)2022-23Replaced 2011-12; first release 15 Jun 2026 (May 2026 data); basket 697 → 957 items; weights: primary articles 22.76%, fuel and power 14.11%, manufactured products 63.13%; released by DPIIT with new Producer Price Indices (PPI); WPI to run for five years
Index of Core Industries (ICI)2022-23Replaced 2011-12; first release 20 Jul 2026 (June 2026 data); nine industries now (Iron Ore added to the earlier eight); top weights: electricity 30.93, refinery products 22.57, steel 17.58
Sensex1978-79 = 100Launched 1986
Nifty 503 Nov 1995 = 1000Launched 22 Apr 1996

Why it exists

  • Annual factory surveys report structural data with substantial time lags unsuitable for immediate monetary and business cycle monitoring.
  • A monthly volume index tracks real industrial activity trends and signals near-term economic performance quickly.

How it works

  • Statistical authorities collect monthly physical production volume reports from designated industrial enterprises across product baskets.
  • Physical outputs are indexed against historical base year volumes and weighted by sectoral gross value added contributions.
  • The composite index is released monthly to evaluate industrial growth across mining, manufacturing, and utility sectors.

More fixed facts

  • Index of Eight Core Industries Feb 2026: +2.3% (Jan +4%); core weight in IIP 40.27%; base 2011-12
  • New IIP basket: 1,042 products in 463 item groups; NIC-2025; adds minor minerals, rare earth minerals, gas supply, water supply, sewerage and waste; separate indices for renewable and non-renewable electricity; released with 28 days' lag

How it changed

  • June 2026: IIP base year 2011-12 → 2022-23; new series from 1 June 2026; TAC chaired by Dr Mridul K. Saggar (IIM Kozhikode)

Traps

  • The index evaluates physical volume of output produced rather than nominal rupee value or turnover.
  • Manufacturing carries the predominant weighting in the basket compared to mining and electric power utilities.

In the news

  • Oct 2026: IIP growth for August 2026
  • Jun 2026: New IIP series (base 2022-23) launched
  • Feb 2026: TAC on IIP base year revision submits report

10. Revision

10.1 Constitution, polity and amendments

Which amendment changed the Preamble and what did it add?
42nd Amendment, 1976: Socialist, Secular, Integrity.
What is the maximum overdue limit in days for standard loan accounts eligible for calamity relief, and what is the effective date?
Accounts not beyond 30 days overdue on the event date, effective 1 Apr 2026.
How many judicial officers, special public prosecutors, and support staff staff each fast track special court?
Each court has 1 judicial officer, 1 special public prosecutor, and 6 support staff.
Original vs current count of Articles, Parts and Schedules?
395, 22, 8 → about 448, 25 (Part VII repealed), 12.
Which Articles form Part III, and what is the ladder of the six rights?
Arts 12–35; Equality 14–18, Freedom 19–22, Exploitation 23–24, Religion 25–28, Cultural-Educational 29–30, Remedies 32 (5-4-2-4-2-1).
Which writ for illegal detention, quashing an order, and challenging the right to hold office?
Habeas Corpus; Certiorari; Quo Warranto.
Art 32 vs Art 226?
Art 32: Supreme Court, Fundamental Rights only. Art 226: High Courts, FRs and other legal rights.
Which Articles did the 42nd Amendment add to the DPSP?
39A (free legal aid), 43A (workers in management), 48A (environment).
How many Fundamental Duties, and which amendments?
11: ten by the 42nd (1976), the 11th by the 86th (2002).
What are the committee sizes: PAC, COPU, Estimates?
PAC 22 (15 LS + 7 RS); COPU 22; Estimates 30 (Lok Sabha only).
What is the quorum, and which Article provides a joint sitting?
One-tenth of members (Art 100); joint sitting Art 108, presided by the Speaker.
Which Articles, Parts and Schedules do the 73rd, 74th and 97th Amendments relate to?
73rd: Part IX, 11th Schedule (29 subjects). 74th: Part IXA, 12th Schedule (18). 97th: Part IXB (cooperatives).
Which Articles cover National Emergency, President's Rule and Financial Emergency?
352, 356, 360 (financial never used).
What majority is needed for most amendments?
Majority of total membership and two-thirds of those present and voting; federal features also need half of the state legislatures.
Basic structure chain?
Golaknath 1967 (no) → Kesavananda Bharati 1973 (13 judges, basic structure) → Minerva Mills 1980 (reaffirmed).
Which judgments: Sec 66A struck down, privacy a Fundamental Right, electoral bonds struck down?
Shreya Singhal 2015; Puttaswamy 2017 (9 judges); ADR 2024.
Which amendment introduced GST, and which Articles?
101st (2016); Arts 246A, 269A, 279A.
Fifth vs Sixth Schedule?
Fifth: 10 states, President declares Scheduled Areas, advisory TAC. Sixth: Assam, Meghalaya, Tripura, Mizoram, Governor, Autonomous District Councils (26 + 4 = 30).
Modes of acquiring and losing citizenship?
Acquire: Birth, Descent, Registration, Naturalisation, Incorporation. Lose: Renunciation, Termination, Deprivation.
Under which Act is the Census held, and which Entry?
Census Act 1948; Entry 69, Union List.
When is the Census 2027 reference date?
1 Mar 2027 (1 Oct 2026 for snow-bound areas); houselisting from 1 Apr 2026.
Maximum Bharat Ratna and Padma awards a year?
Bharat Ratna 3; Padma 120 (excluding posthumous and foreign / NRI awardees).
Which Article covers Karnataka, Mizoram and Nagaland (special provisions)?
371J; 371G; 371A. Proposed 371K is for Ladakh (not enacted).
Which constitutional status did NCBC get and when?
Art 338B, 102nd Amendment 2018.
Who chairs the NDMA, SDMA, DDMA?
Prime Minister; Chief Minister; District Collector (co-chair an elected representative of the local authority).
When was the Disaster Management Act enacted?
23 Dec 2005 (notified 26 Dec 2005); amended in 2025 (Rajya Sabha passed 25 Mar 2025).
What events led to the 2005 Act?
Pant Committee 1999; Gujarat earthquake 2001 (NDMA recommended); MHA in charge 2002; tsunami 2004.
NCMC and HLC: chairs?
NCMC: Cabinet Secretary. HLC: Union Home Minister. Both statutory since the 2025 amendment.
Which section covers NDRF force, NIDM, UDMA?
Sec 44 (NDRF), Sec 42 (NIDM), Sec 41A (UDMA, 2025).
NDRF and SDRF: legal basis and sharing?
Sec 46 (NDRF) and Sec 48(1)(a) (SDRF); NDRF 100% central; SDRF 75:25, North-East and hill States 90:10.
Which FC merged CRF and NCCF into NDRF and SDRF?
13th Finance Commission (2009).
16th FC disaster provisions?
SDRF + SDMF ₹2,04,401 crore for 2026-31, split 80:20; heatwaves and lightning recommended as notified disasters.
Steps for central relief after a severe calamity?
State memorandum → ministry check → Central Team → SC-NEC → High Level Committee → Finance Ministry releases.
Sendai Framework: dates, targets, priorities?
Adopted 18 Mar 2015 (Sendai); 2015–2030; 7 targets, 4 priorities; replaced Hyogo 2005.
Mission Mausam: purpose and outlay?
Seven-day forecasts for cyclones and floods; ₹2,000 crore; launched 14 Jan 2025 (IMD 150th year).

10.2 RBI, monetary policy and banking

RBI chain: commission, Act, start, Mumbai, nationalisation?
1926 Hilton Young → 1934 Act → 1 Apr 1935 → 1937 Mumbai → 1 Jan 1949.
What is the minimum Stage 2 provision under ECL?
5% minimum.
Under which section and Act was Ways and Means Advances introduced?
Section 17(5) of the RBI Act.
To which benchmark yield is the Floating Rate Bond coupon linked?
The 182-day T-Bill yield.
What was the tenor of the USD/INR buy/sell swap auction conducted in May 2026?
3 years.
Which earlier criminal statute did FEMA 1999 replace?
FERA 1973.
What is the minimum leverage ratio for D-SIBs under Basel III?
4%.
Under which section of the Payment and Settlement Systems Act 2007 is MDR barred on UPI?
Section 10A.
When was wholesale CBDC launched in India?
1 Nov 2022.
What is the minimum net worth requirement for a TReDS platform operator?
₹25 crore.
In which year was FEDAI set up?
1958.
What is the deadline for Account Aggregator network interoperability?
31 Dec 2026.
What is the maximum outstanding balance limit for a general-purpose PPI?
₹2 lakh.
What is the aggregate Capital Market Exposure ceiling for banks?
40% of eligible capital base.
Within how many months of turning NPA must a bank classify a wilful defaulter from 1 April 2027?
6 months.
What is the aggregate ceiling on unsecured loans and advances for UCBs?
20% of total loans and advances.
What was the net foreign direct investment inflow reported for April-December 2025?
$3 bn net FDI.
In which year did the SAARC Currency Swap Framework become operational?
2012.
What was the base target size of Sagarmala Finance Corporation blue bond?
₹600 crore (plus ₹500 crore green shoe).
With how many countries had India established DPI cooperation agreements?
24 countries.
What are the main thematic pillars defined for public sector banking transformation under EASE?
Four themes: Risk and Resilience, Innovation, Socio-economic Impact, Excellence.
Since which year has the Financial Stability Report been published bi-annually?
Published biannual since 2010 (FSDC Sub-Committee).
How many characters make up the Legal Entity Identifier code, and from what year is Section B effective?
Section A LEI has 20 characters, and Section B UTI takes effect from 1 Jan 2027.
By what time must banks disclose bulk deposit rates on each business day, and what is the latest grace time?
Daily disclosure by 10:00 AM each business day, with grace of 10 minutes to latest 10:10 AM.
What weights are assigned to Payment Performance and Payment Enablers in the index?
Payment Performance has a weight of 45%, and Payment Enablers has a weight of 25%.
What is the maximum duration in days for a temporary debit hold, and how many days does the holder get to explain?
Temporary Debit Hold is maximum 60 days, and the account holder gets 20 days to explain.
Under which sections of the Banking Regulation Act 1949 did RBI issue the note authentication directions?
Sections 35A and 56 of the Banking Regulation Act, 1949.
Which meeting of the Central Board approved the surplus transfer to the Central Government in 2026?
The 623rd meeting on 22 May 2026 approved the transfer.
From which date in May 2026 was the IFR requirement discontinued for commercial banks?
Discontinued from 18 May 2026, with balance as on 17 May 2026 moved below the line.
Under which Act from 2007 were the directions on inward cross-border payments issued?
Under Section 10 and Section 18 of the Payment and Settlement Systems Act 2007.
How many performance parameters are set under the DFS Viability Plan for RRBs, and what was the record low date?
DFS Viability Plan has 30 performance parameters, with lowest NPAs on record on 31 March 2026.
How many million shares were allotted in the preferential bank deal, and at what rupee price per share?
Preferential allotment of up to 959 million shares at ₹280 each.
At how many days overdue is the first notice served for restricting device functions, and how many days are given to pay?
First notice when the loan is 60 days overdue, giving at least 21 days to pay.
What is the maximum percentage of acquisition value that banks can finance, and within how many months must bridge finance be replaced by equity?
Bank financing at most 75% of acquisition value, and bridge finance replaced within 12 months.
First Governor and first Indian Governor?
Sir Osborne Smith; C D Deshmukh (1943).
Who is the 26th RBI Governor and since when?
Sanjay Malhotra, from 11 Dec 2024.
RBI Act sections for directions, notes, CRR, MPC?
s.7; s.22; s.42; s.45ZB.
MPC: members, quorum, formation?
6 members (3 RBI + 3 external); quorum 4; constituted 29 Sep 2016; at least 4 meetings a year; minutes after 14 days.
Inflation target and failure?
4% CPI ±2% (valid 1 Apr 2026 to 31 Mar 2031); failure = outside the band for 3 consecutive quarters.
Rate corridor?
SDF = repo − 0.25 (floor); MSF = repo + 0.25 (ceiling); Bank Rate = MSF; width 50 bps.
CRR vs SLR?
CRR: cash with RBI (s.42 RBI Act). SLR: with the bank itself, ceiling 40% (s.24 BR Act).
M3?
M1 + time deposits with banks; money multiplier = M3 ÷ M0.
Order and years of lending-rate regimes?
BPLR 2003 → Base Rate 1 Jul 2010 → MCLR 1 Apr 2016 → EBLR 1 Oct 2019.
Nationalisation dates and numbers?
19 Jul 1969: 14 banks (deposits above ₹50 crore); 15 Apr 1980: 6 banks.
Which four anchor banks absorbed others in 2020?
PNB, Canara, Union Bank, Indian Bank; 12 PSBs remain.
NBFC layers and the cut-off?
Base, Middle, Upper, Top (empty); SBR from 1 Oct 2022; Base cut-off ₹1,000 crore.
Banking Laws (Amendment) Act 2025: three headline changes?
Nominees 1 → 4; substantial interest ₹5 lakh → ₹2 crore; co-operative bank director tenure 8 → 10 years.
Bankers' Books Evidence Act 2026?
Replaces the 1891 Act; in force 1 Oct 2026; digital records admissible.
RRB ownership and count?
Centre 50 : State 15 : Sponsor bank 35; 28 RRBs from 1 May 2025; first five on 2 Oct 1975.
India's Basel III minimums?
CET1 5.5%; Tier 1 7%; CRAR 9%; CCB 2.5% → 11.5%.
When do the Basel III market-risk directions apply?
1 Apr 2027 (issued 21 Sep 2026).
NPA definition and SMA stages?
Overdue more than 90 days; SMA-0 1–30, SMA-1 31–60, SMA-2 61–90 days.
Provisioning ladder?
Sub-standard 15% (secured) / 25% (unsecured); doubtful 25 → 40 → 100%; loss 100%.
Recovery thresholds: DRT and IBC?
DRT ₹20 lakh and above; IBC default ₹1 crore and above (330 days).
PSL targets?
40% of ANBC/CEOBE: agriculture 18, small and marginal farmers 10, micro 7.5, weaker sections 12; RRB 75; SFB 60.
When did UPI and RTGS start?
UPI 11 Apr 2016; RTGS 2004 (minimum ₹2 lakh).
Which note does the Finance Ministry issue?
₹1 note (signed by the Finance Secretary).
UPI zero-charge limit and its legal hook?
Up to ₹2,000; Section 10A, PSS Act 2007.
Who chairs the Payments Regulatory Board and from when?
RBI Governor; effective 9 May 2025; replaced BPSS.
DICGC cover and payout time?
₹5 lakh per depositor per bank (since 4 Feb 2020); within 90 days; risk-based premium from 1 Apr 2026.
Integrated Ombudsman Scheme launch?
12 Nov 2021 ("One Nation One Ombudsman").
FERA vs FEMA; LRS limit?
FERA 1973 criminal; FEMA 1999 civil (effective 1 Jun 2000); LRS $250,000 per person per year.
RBI ECL norms: stage provisions and start date?
Stage 1 0.4%, Stage 2 5%, Stage 3 full lifetime loss; from 1 Apr 2027; transition spread to 31 Mar 2031.
What does ECL replace?
The incurred-loss IRAC ladder; a Repeal Directions 2026 ends the old framework (issued 27 Apr 2026).
Basel III credit-risk directions: key numbers?
From 1 Apr 2027; 150% on unrated corporates above ₹500 crore; regulatory retail limit ₹7.5 → ₹10 crore; AD&C borrower contribution 25%.
Under which Section did RBI cancel Paytm Payments Bank's licence?
Section 22(4), Banking Regulation Act 1949, effective 24 Apr 2026.
Payments banks: minimum capital, first to launch, number?
₹100 crore; Airtel (2017); IPPB 1 Sep 2018; five banks (Airtel, IPPB, Fino, Jio, NSDL).
SFB to universal bank: eligibility?
Scheduled, 5-year record, listed, net worth ₹1,000 crore, GNPA ≤ 3%, NNPA ≤ 1%; only AU SFB so far.
Maximum dividend payout for banks?
75% of PAT for commercial, payments and small finance banks; 80% for LABs and RRBs (from FY27).
Loans against securities: LTV and cap?
Shares 60%, equity MFs 75%, debt MFs 85%; ₹1 crore cap per individual; from 1 Apr 2026.
e-mandate no-OTP limits?
₹15,000 per transaction (₹1 lakh for insurance, SIPs, card bills); 24-hour pre-debit notice.
RBI Ombudsman compensation limit?
Up to ₹30 lakh for consequential loss plus ₹3 lakh for harassment.
Utkarsh 2029: period, pillars, deliverables?
2026-29; 6 pillars; 49 deliverables; released 10 Apr 2026.
RBI Digital Payments Index base?
March 2018 = 100.
Index base years now?
CPI 2024; GDP, IIP, WPI and ICI 2022-23; ICI now has nine industries (Iron Ore added).
First release of new IIP and new WPI?
IIP 1 Jun 2026 (April data); WPI 15 Jun 2026 (May data).
RB-IOS 2026: effective date and compensation?
1 Jul 2026; up to ₹30 lakh plus ₹3 lakh; file within 90 days.
Who is in the NBFC Middle Layer?
All deposit-taking NBFCs; others with assets ₹1,000 crore+; SPD, IDF-NBFC, CIC, HFC, NBFC-IFC. Not NBFC-AA.
NBFC Upper Layer threshold?
Assets of ₹1 lakh crore and above, reviewed every three years.
Minimum CRAR for commercial banks, SFBs/payments banks?
9% for commercial banks; 15% for SFBs and payments banks; NBFC ML and above 15%.
Leverage ratio minimums?
D-SIB 4%; other banks 3.5%; AIFI 4%.
UCB tier limits?
Tier 1 up to ₹100 crore; Tier 2 up to ₹1,000 crore; Tier 3 up to ₹10,000 crore; Tier 4 above.
Bank fraud compensation rule (2026)?
Loss up to ₹50,000: 85% or ₹25,000, whichever is lower.
UPI Lite limits?
₹1,000 per transaction; ₹10,000 a day; ₹5,000 balance.
Credit card closure rule?
7 working days; ₹500 a day penalty to cardholder.
What is Mission SAKSHAM?
RBI mission launched 28 Apr 2026 to build capacity of urban co-operative banks (Sahkari Bank Kshamta Nirman).
NSFI 2025–30: release and objectives?
Released 1 Dec 2025 by RBI Governor; five objectives (Panch-Jyoti); 47 action points.
FI-Index weights?
Access 35%, Usage 45%, Quality 20%; launched 17 Aug 2021.
WMA limit for April–September 2026?
₹2,50,000 crore.
RBI surplus to the Government for 2025-26?
₹2,86,588.46 crore.
Financial Literacy Week 2026 dates and theme?
9–13 Feb 2026; theme KYC; every year since 2016.

10.3 GST, taxation and budget

GST milestones: Act, Council, launch?
101st Amendment 2016 (assent 8 Sep); Council constituted 12 Sep 2016; launched 1 Jul 2017.
Under which Constitutional Amendment Act was the GST Council created?
101st Amendment Act, 2016.
What amount was planned for Sovereign Green Bonds in the H2 borrowing plan?
₹15,000 cr Sovereign Green Bonds.
In which financial year was the first Economic Survey presented?
1950-51.
What minimum domestic content requirement was set for BESS under the VGF scheme?
Minimum 40% domestic content requirement.
GST Council voting?
Centre 1/3, States 2/3; decisions need 3/4 of weighted votes.
Four GST taxes?
CGST, SGST, IGST, UTGST (IGST on inter-state supply and imports).
GST 2.0 slabs and date?
5 / 18 / 40 (special); 56th Council 3 Sep 2025; effective 22 Sep 2025; insurance premiums Nil.
Which boards administer direct and indirect taxes?
CBDT (direct); CBIC (indirect: GST and customs).
Income-tax Act 2025: date and changes?
In force 1 Apr 2026; sections 819 → 536; chapters 47 → 23; one "Tax Year".
LTCG and STCG on listed equity?
LTCG 12.5% above ₹1.25 lakh (held more than 12 months); STCG 20%.
Customs Tariff Act sections for safeguard, CVD, ADD?
8B; 9; 9A.
Budget Articles 112 to 117?
112 Budget; 113 demands; 114 Appropriation Bill; 115 supplementary grants; 116 vote on account; 117 Financial Bills.
Fiscal deficit and primary deficit?
Fiscal = total expenditure − (revenue receipts + non-debt capital receipts); primary = fiscal − interest.
Types of cut motion?
Policy cut (₹1), economy cut, token cut (₹100).
N K Singh Committee: key numbers?
Debt 60% of GDP (Centre 40 + States 20); fiscal deficit 3%; escape clause 0.5%.
What is the Economic Survey's legal status?
Convention, not law; tabled one day before the Budget; first in 1950-51.

10.4 Financial regulators and institutions

Which regulator for banks, markets, insurance, pensions?
RBI, SEBI, IRDAI, PFRDA (B-M-I-P).
What unitholder approval does a fresh DR issue on REIT or InvIT units require?
At least 75% of votes cast by unitholders.
What is the revised KYC review periodicity for Category I FPIs from non-high-risk jurisdictions?
8 years (extended from 3 years).
What is the maximum daily penalty cap for commodity derivative position limit violations up to 2%?
₹10,000 cap.
What is the revised minimum investment for individuals in Social Impact Funds?
₹1,000 (reduced from ₹2 lakh).
What is the effective date of the SEBI Portfolio Managers Regulations 2026?
1 Apr 2027 (replacing the 2020 rules).
In which year was the statutory PFRDA Act passed?
2013 under PFRDA Act (in force Feb 2014).
What is the moratorium period under health insurance rules?
5 years (reduced from 8 years).
What is the minimum public shareholding requirement for IFSC-listed companies?
10% (reduced from 25%).
Under which year's Act does FIU-IND work?
2002 under PMLA.
How many industries are covered under the revised Index of Core Industries base 2022-23?
9 industries (expanded from 8 with Iron Ore added).
How many weighted items are included in the Consumer Price Index on base 2024?
358 weighted items (expanded from 299 earlier).
In which financial year quarter did real GDP reach ₹81 lakh crore?
Q1 FY27 (real GDP ₹81 lakh crore).
How many tonnes of gold did China, Poland, and Russia purchase or sell in Q3 2026?
China bought 63 t (largest buyer), Poland bought 41 t (2nd), and Russia sold 19 t.
What is the monetary ceiling in lakh for fast-track settlement under SEBI regulations?
Fast-track settlement covers amounts up to ₹10 lakh.
Under which section of the Companies Act 2013 are Nidhi companies governed, and how many declared Nidhis exist?
Section 406 of the Companies Act 2013, with 395 declared Nidhis.
Within how many working days of offer opening must open-market buybacks be completed under the proposals?
Complete within 66 working days of offer opening, with at least 40% used in the first half.
In what year were the SEBI Index Providers Regulations notified, and what is the significant index AUM threshold in crore?
Regulations 2024, with significant index threshold of ₹1,000 crore.
What capital adequacy in crore is required for Category 1 merchant bankers by January 2028?
Capital adequacy of ₹50 crore by 2 Jan 2028.
Who chairs the FSRASC and who approves heads?
Cabinet Secretary chairs; the Appointments Committee of the Cabinet approves.
SEBI: set up, statutory, replaced which office?
12 Apr 1988; statutory 30 Jan 1992 (SEBI Act); replaced the Controller of Capital Issues.
Who is SEBI Chairperson, and who was the first woman?
Tuhin Kanta Pandey (from 1 Mar 2025); Madhabi Puri Buch.
Which three Acts would the Securities Markets Code 2025 replace?
SCRA 1956, SEBI Act 1992, Depositories Act 1996 (still a Bill).
IRDAI origin and HQ?
Malhotra Committee 1993 → IRDA Act 1999 → IRDAI 2000; Hyderabad.
FDI limit ladder in insurance?
26% (2000) → 49% (2015) → 74% (2021) → 100% (2025).
Which three laws does the 2025 insurance Act amend?
Insurance Act 1938, LIC Act 1956, IRDA Act 1999.
PFRDA and NPS dates?
PFRDA Act 2013 (interim 2003); NPS 2004 (government), 2009 (all); APY 2015; UPS 1 Apr 2025.
Set-up years and HQs: NABARD, EXIM, NHB, SIDBI?
NABARD 12 Jul 1982 Mumbai; EXIM 1 Jan 1982 Mumbai; NHB 9 Jul 1988 New Delhi; SIDBI 2 Apr 1990 Lucknow.
Newest AIFI?
NaBFID (2021), Act 2021, Mumbai.
IFSCA and IBBI?
IFSCA: 2020, GIFT City, Act 2019. IBBI: from Oct 2016 under IBC, New Delhi.
NSDL vs CDSL?
NSDL 1996 (NSE-promoted); CDSL 1999 (BSE-promoted); Depositories Act 1996.
How did LIC come to own IDBI Bank?
Acquired 51% on 21 Jan 2019 (₹21,624 crore); recategorised as private sector bank.
Minimum public shareholding?
25% (Rule 19(2)(b), SCRR 1957); IFSC 10%; amended 13 Mar 2026.
Who are the D-SIIs?
LIC, GIC Re and New India Assurance (FY2025-26).
Since when do insurers follow Ind AS?
1 Apr 2026; parallel reporting for 2 years.
Sabka Bima Sabki Raksha Act: in force and key thresholds?
In force 5 Feb 2026; IRDAI approval threshold 1% → 5%; foreign reinsurer NOF ₹5,000 → ₹1,000 crore.
Health insurance moratorium period?
5 years (60 months), earlier 8; 30-day free look.
NPS Vatsalya: launch and minimum contribution?
18 Sep 2024; ₹250 a year; for minors via guardian.
Multi Cap fund minimum allocation (SEBI)?
75% equity with at least 25% each in large, mid and small cap.
Which body is the SRO for forex dealers?
FEDAI (set up 1958), recognised by RBI as a Self-Regulatory Organisation.
SEBI nomination rule from 1 Sep 2026?
Mandatory for single accounts/folios unless opt-out; up to 3 nominees; optional for joint holders.
MITRA: what is an inactive MF folio?
No investor-initiated transaction for 10 years but units available.
SWAGAT-FI registration period?
10 years (regular FPIs: 3 years).
Technical Glitch Framework change?
Applies to brokers with more than 10,000 clients; report time 1 hour → 2 hours.
D-SIIs for FY 2025-26?
LIC, New India Assurance, GIC Re.

Which Article constitutes the Finance Commission, and which has its report laid?
Art 280; Art 281.
What was the rural LFPR reported in the PLFS?
58% rural LFPR.

Over 54% of manufacturing GVA.

2010.
States' share ladder?
13th 32%; 14th 42%; 15th 41%; 16th 41%.

Arvind Panagariya; 2026-27 to 2030-31; tabled 1 Feb 2026.

C Rangarajan (12th) and Y V Reddy (14th).
Narasimham I and II years?

ADB: Manila, 1966. AIIB: Beijing, 2016.
HQ of BIS, WTO, OECD, FATF, OPEC, SAARC, ASEAN?
Basel; Geneva; Paris; Paris; Vienna; Kathmandu; Jakarta.
UNSC: composition and veto?
15 members: P5 with veto (China, France, Russia, UK, US) + 10 non-permanent on 2-year terms.
UNSC 2026–27 members?
Bahrain, Colombia, DR Congo, Latvia, Liberia.
Ramsar Convention years and India's count?
1971 adopted; 1975 in force; India joined 1982; 103 sites (15 Oct 2026).
Dates: Civil Services Day, Panchayati Raj Day, Income Tax Day, National Consumer Day?
21 Apr; 24 Apr; 24 Jul; 24 Dec.
October day chain?
9 Post; 13 Disaster Risk Reduction; 16 Food; 17 Poverty; 24 UN; 31 Savings (and National Unity).
Which country left OPEC and OPEC+, and when?
The UAE, effective 1 May 2026 (joined OPEC in 1967).
OPEC founders and HQ?
Iran, Iraq, Kuwait, Saudi Arabia, Venezuela; founded at Baghdad 1960; HQ Vienna.
Set-up dates: SAARC, SCO, FAO, WHO?
SAARC 8 Dec 1985 (Dhaka); SCO 15 Jun 2001 (Shanghai); FAO 16 Oct 1945 (Rome); WHO 7 Apr 1948 (Geneva).
Siachen Day, Shaurya Diwas, Maritime Day dates?
13 Apr; 9 Apr; 5 Apr.
Central Excise Day and Janaushadhi Diwas?
24 Feb (Central Excise Act 1944); 7 Mar (PMBI).
UNFCCC, Kyoto, Paris: adopted and in force?
1992 / force 21 Mar 1994; 1997 / force 16 Feb 2005; 12 Dec 2015 / force 4 Nov 2016.
Where is India's only COP?
COP8, New Delhi, 2002 (Delhi Declaration). India withdrew its COP33 (2028) bid.
COP31: venue and dates?
Antalya, Türkiye; 9–20 Nov 2026.
IPCC: year, founders, HQ?
1988; WMO and UNEP; Geneva; Nobel Peace Prize 2007.
Green Climate Fund created at which COP?
COP16, Cancun, 2010.
When was the NAPCC launched and how many missions?
30 Jun 2008; 8 original missions, 9 with Climate Change and Human Health.
India's 2035 NDC targets?
−47% emission intensity; 60% non-fossil capacity; 3.5–4.0 bn t CO₂e sink; net-zero 2070.
India's first NDC (2015) targets?
33–35% intensity cut by 2030; about 40% non-fossil capacity; 2.5–3 bn t sink.
EU CBAM: sectors and full implementation?
Steel, cement, aluminium, fertilisers, electricity, hydrogen; fully in force 1 Jan 2026.
CCTS: entities covered?
About 490: 282 (Oct 2025) + 208 (13 Jan 2026).
When was Beti Bachao Beti Padhao launched, and what is its current umbrella scheme?
22 Jan 2015; merged into Mission Shakti (2021), SAMBAL vertical. SRB 918 (2014-15) → 929 (2024-25).
IFSCA: set-up, law, HQ?
27 Apr 2020; IFSCA Act 2019; GIFT City, Gujarat; combines RBI, SEBI, IRDAI and PFRDA roles for the IFSC.
NHB: set-up date and authorised capital?
9 Jul 1988; ₹1,450 crore, held by the Government.
Where are India's four note-printing presses?
Mysuru and Salboni (BRBNMPL); Nashik and Dewas (SPMCIL).
LIC: founded and Government stake?
1 Sep 1956; 96.5% Government holding.
Who is the 27th Chief of Naval Staff?
Admiral Krishna Swaminathan (took over 31 May 2026).
Current FATF President?
Giles Thomson (UK) from July 2026; before him Elisa de Anda Madrazo.
ASEAN members and India's status?
11 members (Timor-Leste, 2025); India is not a member; Philippines chairs in 2026.
SCO: members and when India joined?
10 members; India and Pakistan joined 2017; Iran 2023; Belarus 2024.
IORA: set-up, HQ and members?
7 Mar 1997; Ebène, Mauritius; 23 members + 12 dialogue partners.
IBCA: HQ, members and Indian funding?
New Delhi; 27 members; ₹150 crore (2023–28).
NDB: president and recent members?
Dilma Rousseff; Algeria (2025), Uzbekistan (2026).
ADB: members, president, India's share?
69 members; Masato Kanda; India 6.3%; Japan and USA 15.6% each.
Number of EU members using the euro?
21 of 27; Denmark has an opt-out.
Loktak Lake: location and Ramsar year?
Moirang, Manipur; Ramsar 1990; Montreux Record 1993.
Jnanpith Award: established and prize?
1961; ₹11 lakh + Vagdevi bronze; Eighth Schedule languages.

10.8 Older important current affairs

Demonetisation and GST dates?
8 Nov 2016; 1 Jul 2017.
In which year did the RoDTEP export remission scheme begin?
2021.
What is the ceiling on Chinese shareholding allowed under the automatic route under revised FEMA guidelines?
Up to 10%.
What is the disinvestment and asset monetisation target set for FY27?
₹80,000 crore.
In which year did India last hold an A category sovereign rating before JCR upgrade?
1988 under Moody's.
Yes Bank reconstruction numbers?
Moratorium 5 Mar 2020; cap ₹50,000; SBI up to 49%; invested ₹7,250 crore; 3-year lock-in; lifted 18 Mar 2020.
Corporate tax cut of Sep 2019?
115BAA 22% (effective 25.17%); 115BAB 15% (effective 17.16%).
LIC IPO?
Listed 17 May 2022; issue price ₹949; about 3.5% stake; ₹20,557 crore.
G20 Presidency of India and the key outcome?
1 Dec 2022 to 30 Nov 2023; summit 9–10 Sep 2023; African Union became the 21st member.
Chandrayaan-3 dates?
Launched 14 Jul 2023; landed 23 Aug 2023.
BRICS members added from 1 Jan 2024?
Egypt, Ethiopia, Iran, UAE.
Article 370 and new criminal codes?
370 abrogated 5 Aug 2019; BNS, BNSS, BSA replaced IPC, CrPC, Evidence Act on 1 Jul 2024.
PNB–Nirav Modi fraud?
Feb 2018; about ₹11,000 crore; led to the Fugitive Economic Offenders Act 2018.
Which trade agreements are with EFTA, UAE, Australia, UK, Oman, New Zealand, and in-force dates?
EFTA TEPA 1 Oct 2025; UAE CEPA 1 May 2022; Australia ECTA 29 Dec 2022; UK CETA 15 Jul 2026; Oman CEPA 1 Jun 2026; NZ 20 Oct 2026.
Status of the India–EU FTA?
Concluded and announced 27 Jan 2026; awaiting signing and approvals.
Exercises: Malabar, Garuda, Nomadic Elephant, Konkan, Surya Kiran?
Malabar: US, Japan (and Australia), Navy; Garuda: France, Air Force; Nomadic Elephant: Mongolia; Konkan: UK, Navy; Surya Kiran: Nepal.
